    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>

        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>

                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>

                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/tutorials/">Tutorials on&nbsp;gimp.org</a></p>

<p><a href="/books/">Books about <span class="caps">GIMP</span></a></p>

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/tutorials/">Tutorials on&nbsp;gimp.org</a></p>

<p><a href="/books/">Books about <span class="caps">GIMP</span></a></p>

<p><a href="userfaq.html">Frequently Asked&nbsp;Questions</a></p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>

                <h1>Documentation</h1>
                
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/tutorials/">Tutorials on&nbsp;gimp.org</a></p>
<h2 id="books">Books<a class="headerlink" href="#books" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/books/">Books about <span class="caps">GIMP</span></a></p>
<h2 id="gimp-user-faq"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User <span class="caps">FAQ</span><a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-faq" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="userfaq.html">Frequently Asked&nbsp;Questions</a></p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/tutorials/">Tutorials on&nbsp;gimp.org</a></p>

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/tutorials/">Tutorials on&nbsp;gimp.org</a></p>

<p><a href="/books/">Books about <span class="caps">GIMP</span></a></p>

<p><a href="userfaq.html">Frequently Asked&nbsp;Questions</a></p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/tutorials/">Tutorials on&nbsp;gimp.org</a></p>

<h2 id="gimp-user-faq"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User <span class="caps">FAQ</span><a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-faq" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="userfaq.html">Frequently Asked&nbsp;Questions</a></p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>

<p><a href="userfaq.html">Frequently Asked&nbsp;Questions</a></p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>

                </div>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

            <div class="column full">
                <h1>Documentation</h1>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/tutorials/">Tutorials on&nbsp;gimp.org</a></p>
<h2 id="books">Books<a class="headerlink" href="#books" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/books/">Books about <span class="caps">GIMP</span></a></p>
<h2 id="gimp-user-faq"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User <span class="caps">FAQ</span><a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-faq" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="userfaq.html">Frequently Asked&nbsp;Questions</a></p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/tutorials/">Tutorials on&nbsp;gimp.org</a></p>
<h2 id="books">Books<a class="headerlink" href="#books" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/books/">Books about <span class="caps">GIMP</span></a></p>
<h2 id="gimp-user-faq"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User <span class="caps">FAQ</span><a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-faq" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="userfaq.html">Frequently Asked&nbsp;Questions</a></p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>

<p><a href="userfaq.html">Frequently Asked&nbsp;Questions</a></p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

    <section class='page_content'>
        <div class='container'>
            <div class='row clearfix'>
                <div class='column full'>
                    <h2 id="gimp-user-manual"><span class="caps">GIMP</span> User Manual<a class="headerlink" href="#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
<h2 id="tutorials">Tutorials<a class="headerlink" href="#tutorials" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><a href="/tutorials/">Tutorials on&nbsp;gimp.org</a></p>

<p><a href="/books/">Books about <span class="caps">GIMP</span></a></p>

<p><a href="userfaq.html">Frequently Asked&nbsp;Questions</a></p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

</html>https://www.gimp.org/docs#gimp-user-manual" title="Permanent link">&para;</a></h2>
<p><span class="caps">GIMP</span> comes with a built-in help system, available in dozens of languages. Once
you have started the program, press <kbd>F1</kbd> for context-sensitive help.
You may have to install the help pages from a separate package (<code>gimp-help</code>),
depending on how your version of <span class="caps">GIMP</span> was&nbsp;packaged.</p>
<p>The manual is also available online on a <a href="https://docs.gimp.org/">dedicated website</a>.</p>
